{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01BweEPJXd6DCfsQdksPzafe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_016DDHWkn2ZNa7sGRTAuCqSF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/d3v6-bubble-chart.md","content":"# D3.js v6 Bubble Chart with Force Simulation\n\n## Overview\nCreating interactive bubble charts using D3.js v6 with force-directed layout, where bubbles are clustered by categories.\n\n## Installation\nDownload D3.js v6 from CDN or official build:\n```html\n<script src=\"https://d3js.org/d3.v6.min.js\"></script>\n```\n\n## Core Concepts\n\n### Force Simulation for Clustering\nD3's force simulation organizes data into clusters by applying forces:\n- **forceSimulation()**: Creates simulation with nodes\n- **forceX()/forceY()**: Attracts nodes toward specific positions based on properties\n- **forceCollide()**: Prevents node overlap with radius-based collision detection\n- **forceManyBody()**: Repulsive force between all nodes\n\n### Basic Bubble Chart Structure\n```javascript\n// 1. Create SVG container\nconst svg = d3.select(\"#chart\")\n  .append(\"svg\")\n  .attr(\"width\", width)\n  .attr(\"height\", height);\n\n// 2. Prepare data with computed properties\nconst data = stocks.map(d => ({\n  ...d,\n  radius: Math.sqrt(d.marketCap) / scale // Size by market cap\n}));\n\n// 3. Create simulation\nconst simulation = d3.forceSimulation(data)\n  .force(\"x\", d3.forceX(d => xScale(d.sector)).strength(0.5))\n  .force(\"y\", d3.forceY(height / 2).strength(0.1))\n  .force(\"collide\", d3.forceCollide(d => d.radius + padding))\n  .force(\"charge\", d3.forceManyBody().strength(-30));\n\n// 4. Create visual elements\nconst circles = svg.selectAll(\"circle\")\n  .data(data, d => d.ticker)\n  .enter()\n  .append(\"circle\")\n  .attr(\"r\", d => d.radius)\n  .attr(\"fill\", d => colorScale(d.sector));\n\n// 5. Update positions on simulation tick\nsimulation.on(\"tick\", () => {\n  circles.attr(\"cx\", d => d.x).attr(\"cy\", d => d.y);\n});\n```\n\n## Key Parameters for Clustering\n\n### Force Strength\n```javascript\n// Stronger clustering: cluster closer together\n.force(\"x\", d3.forceX(d => xScale(d.sector)).strength(0.7))\n\n// Weaker clustering: more spread out\n.force(\"x\", d3.forceX(d => xScale(d.sector)).strength(0.3))\n```\n\n### Collision Padding\n```javascript\n// Prevent overlapping with padding\n.force(\"collide\", d3.forceCollide(d => d.radius + 5))\n```\n\n### Charge Force (Repulsion)\n```javascript\n// Stronger repulsion: nodes push each other away\n.force(\"charge\", d3.forceManyBody().strength(-50))\n\n// Weaker repulsion: nodes can be closer\n.force(\"charge\", d3.forceManyBody().strength(-10))\n```\n\n## Tooltips and Interaction\n```javascript\nconst tooltip = d3.select(\"body\")\n  .append(\"div\")\n  .attr(\"class\", \"tooltip\")\n  .style(\"opacity\", 0);\n\ncircles.on(\"mouseover\", function(event, d) {\n  tooltip.transition().duration(200).style(\"opacity\", 0.9);\n  tooltip.html(`${d.ticker}<br/>${d.name}<br/>${d.sector}`)\n    .style(\"left\", (event.pageX + 10) + \"px\")\n    .style(\"top\", (event.pageY - 28) + \"px\");\n})\n.on(\"mouseout\", function() {\n  tooltip.transition().duration(500).style(\"opacity\", 0);\n});\n```\n\n## Text Labels in Bubbles\n```javascript\nconst labels = svg.selectAll(\"text\")\n  .data(data, d => d.ticker)\n  .enter()\n  .append(\"text\")\n  .attr(\"text-anchor\", \"middle\")\n  .attr(\"dy\", \"0.35em\")\n  .attr(\"font-size\", d => Math.max(10, d.radius / 2) + \"px\")\n  .text(d => d.ticker)\n  .style(\"pointer-events\", \"none\"); // Don't intercept mouse events\n\nsimulation.on(\"tick\", () => {\n  labels.attr(\"x\", d => d.x).attr(\"y\", d => d.y);\n});\n```\n\n## Color Scales by Category\n```javascript\nconst colorScale = d3.scaleOrdinal()\n  .domain([...new Set(data.map(d => d.sector))])\n  .range(d3.schemeCategory10); // Or custom colors\n```\n\n## Legend\n```javascript\nconst sectors = [...new Set(data.map(d => d.sector))];\nconst legendY = 20;\n\nsectors.forEach((sector, i) => {\n  svg.append(\"circle\")\n    .attr(\"cx\", 10)\n    .attr(\"cy\", legendY + i * 20)\n    .attr(\"r\", 5)\n    .attr(\"fill\", colorScale(sector));\n\n  svg.append(\"text\")\n    .attr(\"x\", 20)\n    .attr(\"y\", legendY + i * 20 + 5)\n    .text(sector)\n    .style(\"font-size\", \"12px\");\n});\n```\n\n## Performance Tips\n- Use `d3.transition()` for smooth animations\n- Bind data with keys to handle dynamic updates\n- Use `.style(\"pointer-events\", \"none\")` on non-interactive elements\n- Limit simulation iterations with `.alpha()` and `.alphaDecay()`\n- Consider canvas instead of SVG for very large datasets (100+ nodes)\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n- Forgetting to update text labels with circles during simulation\n- Setting force strength too high (nodes oscillate wildly)\n- Not accounting for margin when rendering clusters\n- Missing collision force causes overlapping bubbles\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":248,"cache_creation_input_tokens":258,"cache_read_input_tokens":20095,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":258,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"72c189fa-d551-409c-b661-6278d6e9803c","uuid":"80b71a9e-7769-44d8-80c1-9f49e5f21de1"}

// Array of objects\n  // {ticker: \"AAPL\", sector: \"Tech\", marketCap: 2500000000000}\n});\n```\n\n**Important**: D3.csv automatically converts numeric strings to numbers if they parse correctly.\n\n## Data Type Conversion\n```javascript\n// Manual type conversion\nconst data = d3.csv(\"file.csv\").then(data => {\n  return data.map(d => ({\n    ticker: d.ticker,\n    marketCap: +d.marketCap, // Force number\n    sector: d.sector,\n    employees: +d.fullTimeEmployees || 0 // Default fallback\n  }));\n});\n```\n\n## Handling Missing Values\n```javascript\nconst cleanData = data.map(d => ({\n  ...d,\n  marketCap: d.marketCap ? +d.marketCap : null,\n  yield: d.yield === \"\" ? null : +d.yield\n}));\n\n// Filter out entries with missing critical fields\nconst validStocks = cleanData.filter(d => d.marketCap !== null);\n```\n\n## Creating Data From Multiple Sources\n```javascript\n// Combine description and price history\nconst stockDescriptions = d3.csv(\"stock-descriptions.csv\");\nconst priceHistories = {}; // Load as needed\n\nPromise.all([stockDescriptions]).then(([descriptions]) => {\n  // For each description, load corresponding price history\n  return Promise.all(\n    descriptions.map(desc =>\n      d3.csv(`indiv-stock/${desc.ticker}.csv`)\n        .then(prices => {\n          priceHistories[desc.ticker] = prices;\n          return desc;\n        })\n    )\n  );\n}).then(stocks => {\n  // Now we have descriptions and prices\n});\n```\n\n## CSV in HTML\nYou can embed CSV as a data URI or include it in a script tag:\n```html\n<script type=\"text/csv\" id=\"stock-data\">\nticker,name,sector\nAAPL,Apple,Technology\n...\n</script>\n\n<script>\nconst csv = document.getElementById(\"stock-data\").textContent;\nPapa.parse(csv, {\n  header: true,\n  complete: (results) => {\n    // Process data\n  }\n});\n</script>\n```\n\n## Copying CSV Files to Output\nFor a self-contained web app:\n```bash\n# Copy all data files\ncp -r /root/data/* /root/output/data/\n```\n\nThen load in browser:\n```javascript\nd3.csv(\"data/stock-descriptions.csv\").then(data => {\n  // Process\n});\n```\n\n## Format Guidelines\n- CSV files should have headers in the first row\n- Use consistent delimiters (comma by default)\n- Quote fields containing commas: `\"Name, Inc.\"`\n- Numeric fields will be auto-detected by D3.js\n- Use empty string for missing values, not \"N/A\"\n\n## Performance Considerations\n- Loading 50 stocks + 50 price histories is feasible (~2-5 MB total)\n- Lazy-load price histories only when needed\n- Cache loaded data in memory to avoid re-parsing\n- Use async/await for cleaner promise chains\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1962,"cache_read_input_tokens":20353,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1962,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"72c189fa-d551-409c-b661-6278d6e9803c","uuid":"500c5a1c-a5e9-4d89-bdd8-a5b0f842a3c9"}

Interactive Data Synchronization Between Chart and Table\n\n## Overview\nSynchronizing selection state between D3.js visualizations and HTML tables through event handlers and CSS classes.\n\n## Basic Architecture\n```javascript\n// Central state object\nconst state = {\n  selectedTicker: null\n};\n\n// Event handlers\nfunction selectStock(ticker) {\n  state.selectedTicker = ticker;\n  updateChart();\n  updateTable();\n}\n\n// Chart click handler\ncircles.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n  selectStock(d.ticker);\n});\n\n// Table click handler\ntableRows.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n  selectStock(d.ticker);\n});\n```\n\n## Highlighting in Charts\n```javascript\nconst circles = svg.selectAll(\"circle\");\n\nfunction updateChart() {\n  circles.style(\"opacity\", d => {\n    if (state.selectedTicker === null) return 1;\n    return d.ticker === state.selectedTicker ? 1 : 0.3;\n  })\n  .style(\"stroke\", d => {\n    if (d.ticker === state.selectedTicker) {\n      return \"#000\";\n    }\n    return \"none\";\n  })\n  .style(\"stroke-width\", 2);\n}\n```\n\n## Highlighting in Tables\n```javascript\nconst tableRows = d3.selectAll(\"tbody tr\");\n\nfunction updateTable() {\n  tableRows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n}\n```\n\n## Syncing Table Scroll to Selection\n```javascript\nfunction updateTable() {\n  tableRows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n\n  // Scroll selected row into view\n  if (state.selectedTicker) {\n    const selectedRow = document.querySelector(`tr[data-ticker=\"${state.selectedTicker}\"]`);\n    if (selectedRow) {\n      selectedRow.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\", block: \"nearest\" });\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Using CSS Classes for Styling\n```css\n/* Default row */\ntr {\n  background: white;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background 0.2s;\n}\n\n/* Hover state */\ntr:hover {\n  background: #f5f5f5;\n}\n\n/* Selected state */\ntr.selected {\n  background: #e3f2fd;\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n\n/* Bubble styling */\ncircle {\n  transition: opacity 0.2s, stroke 0.2s;\n}\n```\n\n## Deselection\n```javascript\n// Clear selection on background click\nsvg.on(\"click\", function(event) {\n  if (event.target === this) {\n    selectStock(null);\n  }\n});\n\n// Also clear on table click outside\ndocument.addEventListener(\"click\", (event) => {\n  if (!event.target.closest(\"tr\") && !event.target.closest(\"circle\")) {\n    selectStock(null);\n  }\n});\n```\n\n## Advanced: Two-Way Data Binding Pattern\n```javascript\nclass StockVisualization {\n  constructor(chartSelector, tableSelector) {\n    this.chart = d3.select(chartSelector);\n    this.table = d3.select(tableSelector);\n    this.selectedTicker = null;\n  }\n\n  render(data) {\n    this.renderChart(data);\n    this.renderTable(data);\n    this.attachEventHandlers();\n  }\n\n  renderChart(data) {\n    // Create circles with selection callback\n    this.circles = this.chart.selectAll(\"circle\")\n      .data(data)\n      .enter()\n      .append(\"circle\")\n      .on(\"click\", (event, d) => this.select(d.ticker));\n  }\n\n  renderTable(data) {\n    // Create rows with selection callback\n    this.rows = this.table.selectAll(\"tr\")\n      .data(data)\n      .enter()\n      .append(\"tr\")\n      .on(\"click\", (event, d) => this.select(d.ticker));\n  }\n\n  select(ticker) {\n    this.selectedTicker = ticker;\n    this.updateVisualization();\n  }\n\n  updateVisualization() {\n    this.circles.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === this.selectedTicker);\n    this.rows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === this.selectedTicker);\n\n    // Scroll table\n    if (this.selectedTicker) {\n      const row = this.table.select(`[data-ticker=\"${this.selectedTicker}\"]`).node();\n      if (row) row.scrollIntoView({ block: \"nearest\" });\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Event Delegation for Dynamic Content\n```javascript\n// If table rows are added dynamically\nconst tableBody = document.getElementById(\"stock-table\");\ntableBody.addEventListener(\"click\", (event) => {\n  const row = event.target.closest(\"tr\");\n  if (row) {\n    const ticker = row.dataset.ticker;\n    selectStock(ticker);\n  }\n});\n```\n\n## Handling Multiple Selections\n```javascript\nconst state = {\n  selectedTickers: new Set()\n};\n\nfunction toggleSelection(ticker) {\n  if (state.selectedTickers.has(ticker)) {\n    state.selectedTickers.delete(ticker);\n  } else {\n    state.selectedTickers.add(ticker);\n  }\n  updateVisualization();\n}\n\nfunction updateChart() {\n  circles.classed(\"selected\", d => state.selectedTickers.has(d.ticker));\n}\n```\n\n## Visual Feedback\n```javascript\n// Pulse animation on selection\ncircles.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n Interactive Data Synchronization Between Chart and Table\n\n## Overview\nSynchronizing selection state between D3.js visualizations and HTML tables through event handlers and CSS classes.\n\n## Basic Architecture\n```javascript\n// Central state object\nconst state = {\n  selectedTicker: null\n};\n\n// Event handlers\nfunction selectStock(ticker) {\n  state.selectedTicker = ticker;\n  updateChart();\n  updateTable();\n}\n\n// Chart click handler\ncircles.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n  selectStock(d.ticker);\n});\n\n// Table click handler\ntableRows.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n  selectStock(d.ticker);\n});\n```\n\n## Highlighting in Charts\n```javascript\nconst circles = svg.selectAll(\"circle\");\n\nfunction updateChart() {\n  circles.style(\"opacity\", d => {\n    if (state.selectedTicker === null) return 1;\n    return d.ticker === state.selectedTicker ? 1 : 0.3;\n  })\n  .style(\"stroke\", d => {\n    if (d.ticker === state.selectedTicker) {\n      return updateTable() {\n  tableRows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n}\n```\n\n## Syncing Table Scroll to Selection\n```javascript\nfunction updateTable() {\n  tableRows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n\n  // Scroll selected row into view\n  if (state.selectedTicker) {\n    const selectedRow = document.querySelector(`tr[data-ticker=\"${state.selectedTicker}\"]`);\n    if (selectedRow) {\n      selectedRow.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\", block: \"nearest\" });\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Using CSS Classes for Styling\n```css\n/* Default row */\ntr {\n  background: white;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background 0.2s;\n}\n\n/* Hover state */\ntr:hover {\n  background: #f5f5f5;\n}\n\n/* Selected state */\ntr.selected {\n  background: #e3f2fd;\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n\n/* Bubble styling */\ncircle {\n  transition: opacity 0.2s, stroke 0.2s;\n}\n```\n\n## Deselection\n```javascript\n// Clear selection on background click\nsvg.on(\"click\", function(event) {\n  if (event.target === this) {\n    selectStock(null);\n  }\n});\n\n// Also clear on table click outside\ndocument.addEventListener(\"click\", (event) => {\n  if (!event.target.closest(\"tr\") && !event.target.closest(\"circle\")) {\n    selectStock(null);\n  }\n});\n```\n\n## Advanced: Two-Way Data Binding Pattern\n```javascript\nclass StockVisualization {\n  constructor(chartSelector, tableSelector) {\n    this.chart = d3.select(chartSelector);\n    this.table = d3.select(tableSelector);\n    this.selectedTicker = null;\n  }\n\n  render(data) {\n    this.renderChart(data);\n    this.renderTable(data);\n    this.attachEventHandlers();\n  }\n\n  renderChart(data) {\n    // Create circles with selection callback\n    this.circles = this.chart.selectAll(\"circle\")\n      .data(data)\n      .enter()\n      .append(\"circle\")\n      .on(\"click\", (event, d) => this.select(d.ticker));\n  }\n\n  renderTable(data) {\n    // Create rows with selection callback\n    this.rows = this.table.selectAll(\"tr\")\n      .data(data)\n      .enter()\n      .append(\"tr\")\n      .on(\"click\", (event, d) => this.select(d.ticker));\n  }\n\n  select(ticker) {\n    this.selectedTicker = ticker;\n    this.updateVisualization();\n  }\n\n  updateVisualization() {\n    this.circles.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === this.selectedTicker);\n    this.rows.classed(\"selected\", d => d.ticker === this.selectedTicker);\n\n    // Scroll table\n    if (this.selectedTicker) {\n      const row = this.table.select(`[data-ticker=\"${this.selectedTicker}\"]`).node();\n      if (row) row.scrollIntoView({ block: \"nearest\" });\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Event Delegation for Dynamic Content\n```javascript\n// If table rows are added dynamically\nconst tableBody = selectStock(ticker);\n  }\n});\n```\n\n## Handling Multiple Selections\n```javascript\nconst state = {\n  selectedTickers: new Set()\n};\n\nfunction toggleSelection(ticker) {\n  if (state.selectedTickers.has(ticker)) {\n    state.selectedTickers.delete(ticker);\n  } else {\n    state.selectedTickers.add(ticker);\n  }\n  updateVisualization();\n}\n\nfunction updateChart() {\n  circles.classed(\"selected\", d => state.selectedTickers.has(d.ticker));\n}\n```\n\n## Visual Feedback\n```javascript\n// Pulse animation on selection\ncircles.on(\"click\", (event, d) => {\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01Mjxvwjk3rhtvACezD2rHud","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_0115vZ4zFAHhVsJpbZfyWgqi","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/output/js/visualization.js","content":"// Stock Market Visualization with D3.js v6\n// Bubble chart with force simulation + interactive data table\n\n(function() {\n    'use strict';\n\n    // Global state\n    const state = {\n        selectedTicker: null,\n        stocks: [],\n        stocksByTicker: {}\n    };\n\n    // Configuration\n    const config = {\n        chartWidth: 600,\n        chartHeight: 600,\n        margin: { top: 20, right: 20, bottom: 20, left: 20 },\n        forceStrength: 0.5,\n        forceYStrength: 0.1,\n        chargeStrength: -30,\n        collisionPadding: 5\n    };\n\n    // Color palette for sectors\n    const sectorColors = {\n        'Information Technology': '#1f77b4',\n        'Financials': '#ff7f0e',\n        'Healthcare': '#2ca02c',\n        'Industrials': '#d62728',\n        'Consumer Discretionary': '#9467bd',\n        'Consumer Staples': '#8c564b',\n        'Energy': '#e377c2',\n        'Materials': '#7f7f7f',\n        'Real Estate': '#bcbd22',\n        'Communication Services': '#17becf',\n        'Utilities': '#1a9850',\n        'ETF': '#cccccc'\n    };\n\n    // Get color for sector\n    function getSectorColor(sector) {\n        return sectorColors[sector] || '#999';\n    }\n\n    // Format market cap as human-readable\n    function formatMarketCap(value) {\n        if (!value || value === null || value === undefined) return 'N/A';\n\n        const absValue = Math.abs(value);\n\n        if (absValue >= 1e12) {\n            return (value / 1e12).toFixed(2) + 'T';\n        } else if (absValue >= 1e9) {\n            return (value / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B';\n        } else if (absValue >= 1e6) {\n            return (value / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M';\n        } else if (absValue >= 1e3) {\n            return (value / 1e3).toFixed(2) + 'K';\n        }\n\n        return value.toFixed(2);\n    }\n\n    // Calculate bubble radius based on market cap\n    function calculateRadius(marketCap, allCaps) {\n        if (!marketCap || marketCap === null) {\n            return 25; // Fixed size for ETFs\n        }\n\n        const maxCap = Math.max(...allCaps.filter(m => m && m > 0));\n        const minCap = Math.min(...allCaps.filter(m => m && m > 0));\n        const range = maxCap - minCap;\n\n        const normalized = (marketCap - minCap) / range;\n        const radius = 20 + normalized * 60; // Between 20 and 80 pixels\n        return radius;\n    }\n\n    // Load and process data\n    async function loadData() {\n        try {\n            const stocks = await d3.csv('data/stock-descriptions.csv');\n\n            // Process stock data\n            const processedStocks = stocks.map(d => {\n                const marketCap = d.marketCap ? parseFloat(d.marketCap) : null;\n                return {\n                    ticker: d.ticker,\n                    name: d['full name'] || d.name,\n                    sector: d.sector,\n                    marketCap: marketCap,\n                    country: d.country,\n                    website: d.website,\n                    employees: d.fullTimeEmployees ? parseFloat(d.fullTimeEmployees) : null,\n                    returnOnEquity: d.returnOnEquity ? parseFloat(d.returnOnEquity) : null,\n                    revenuePerShare: d.revenuePerShare ? parseFloat(d.revenuePerShare) : null,\n                    bookValue: d.bookValue ? parseFloat(d.bookValue) : null,\n                    yield: d.yield ? parseFloat(d.yield) : null,\n                    beta: d.beta ? parseFloat(d.beta) : null\n                };\n            });\n\n            // Calculate radii based on market cap\n            const marketCaps = processedStocks.map(s => s.marketCap);\n            processedStocks.forEach(d => {\n                d.radius = calculateRadius(d.marketCap, marketCaps);\n            });\n\n            // Store in state\n            state.stocks = processedStocks;\n            processedStocks.forEach(stock => {\n                state.stocksByTicker[stock.ticker] = stock;\n            });\n\n            return processedStocks;\n        } catch (error) {\n            console.error('Error loading data:', error);\n            return [];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Create bubble chart\n    function createChart(stocks) {\n        // Container dimensions\n        const containerWidth = document.getElementById('chart-container').clientWidth;\n        const containerHeight = config.chartHeight;\n\n        // Calculate inner dimensions\n        const width = containerWidth - config.margin.left - config.margin.right;\n        const height = containerHeight - config.margin.top - config.margin.bottom;\n\n        // Create SVG\n        const svg = d3.select('#chart-container')\n            .append('svg')\n            .attr('width', containerWidth)\n            .attr('height', containerHeight);\n\n        const g = svg.append('g')\n            .attr('transform', `translate(${config.margin.left},${config.margin.top})`);\n\n        // Get unique sectors\n        const sectors = [...new Set(stocks.map(d => d.sector))];\n\n        // Position X centers for each sector (spread across width)\n        const sectorCenters = {};\n        sectors.forEach((sector, i) => {\n            sectorCenters[sector] = (width / (sectors.length + 1)) * (i + 1);\n        });\n\n        // Create force simulation\n        const simulation = d3.forceSimulation(stocks)\n            .force('x', d3.forceX(d => sectorCenters[d.sector]).strength(config.forceStrength))\n            .force('y', d3.forceY(height / 2).strength(config.forceYStrength))\n            .force('collide', d3.forceCollide(d => d.radius + config.collisionPadding))\n            .force('charge', d3.forceManyBody().strength(config.chargeStrength))\n            .alpha(1)\n            .alphaDecay(0.01);\n\n        // Create circles\n        const circles = g.selectAll('circle')\n            .data(stocks, d => d.ticker)\n            .enter()\n            .append('circle')\n            .attr('r', d => d.radius)\n            .attr('fill', d => getSectorColor(d.sector))\n            .attr('cx', d => d.x || width / 2)\n            .attr('cy', d => d.y || height / 2)\n            .attr('data-ticker', d => d.ticker)\n            .on('click', function(event, d) {\n                event.stopPropagation();\n                selectStock(d.ticker);\n            })\n            .on('mouseover', function(event, d) {\n                // Only show tooltip if not ETF (ETF entries don't have marketCap and other data)\n                if (d.marketCap !== null && d.marketCap !== undefined) {\n                    showTooltip(event, d);\n                }\n            })\n            .on('mouseout', hideTooltip);\n\n        // Create text labels\n        const labels = g.selectAll('text')\n            .data(stocks, d => d.ticker)\n            .enter()\n            .append('text')\n            .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')\n            .attr('dy', '0.35em')\n            .attr('x', d => d.x || width / 2)\n            .attr('y', d => d.y || height / 2)\n            .text(d => d.ticker)\n            .style('font-size', d => Math.max(10, d.radius / 3) + 'px');\n\n        // Create tooltip element\n        const tooltip = d3.select('body')\n            .append('div')\n            .attr('class', 'tooltip')\n            .style('opacity', 0);\n\n        function showTooltip(event, d) {\n            tooltip.transition().duration(200).style('opacity', 0.9);\n\n            const tooltipHTML = `\n                <div class=\"tooltip-title\">${d.ticker}</div>\n                <div class=\"tooltip-content\">\n                    <strong>${d.name}</strong><br/>\n                    Sector: ${d.sector}<br/>\n                    Market Cap: ${formatMarketCap(d.marketCap)}\n                </div>\n            `;\n\n            tooltip.html(tooltipHTML)\n                .style('left', (event.pageX + 10) + 'px')\n                .style('top', (event.pageY - 28) + 'px');\n        }\n\n        function hideTooltip() {\n            tooltip.transition().duration(500).style('opacity', 0);\n        }\n\n        // Update positions on simulation tick\n        simulation.on('tick', () => {\n            circles\n                .attr('cx', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(width - d.radius, d.x)))\n                .attr('cy', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(height - d.radius, d.y)));\n\n            labels\n                .attr('x', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(width - d.radius, d.x)))\n                .attr('y', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(height - d.radius, d.y)));\n        });\n\n        // Return elements for later reference\n        return { svg, g, circles, labels, tooltip, width, height };\n    }\n\n    // Create data table\n    function createTable(stocks) {\n        const tbody = d3.select('#stock-table tbody');\n\n        const rows = tbody.selectAll('tr')\n            .data(stocks, d => d.ticker)\n            .enter()\n            .append('tr')\n            .attr('data-ticker', d => d.ticker)\n            .on('click', function(event, d) {\n                selectStock(d.ticker);\n            });\n\n        rows.append('td')\n            .attr('class', 'ticker')\n            .text(d => d.ticker);\n\n        rows.append('td')\n            .attr('class', 'company-name')\n            .title(d => d.name)\n            .text(d => d.name);\n\n        rows.append('td')\n            .attr('class', 'sector')\n            .text(d => d.sector);\n\n        rows.append('td')\n            .attr('class', 'market-cap')\n            .text(d => formatMarketCap(d.marketCap));\n\n        return rows;\n    }\n\n    // Create legend\n    function createLegend(stocks) {\n        const sectors = [...new Set(stocks.map(d => d.sector))].sort();\n\n        const legend = d3.select('#legend-container');\n\n        sectors.forEach(sector => {\n            const item = legend.append('div')\n                .attr('class', 'legend-item');\n\n            item.append('div')\n                .attr('class', 'legend-color')\n                .style('background-color', getSectorColor(sector));\n\n            item.append('span')\n                .text(sector);\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Select/highlight a stock\n    function selectStock(ticker) {\n        // Toggle selection\n        if (state.selectedTicker === ticker) {\n            state.selectedTicker = null;\n        } else {\n            state.selectedTicker = ticker;\n        }\n\n        updateSelection();\n    }\n\n    // Update selection highlighting\n    function updateSelection() {\n        // Update circles\n        d3.selectAll('circle').classed('selected', d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n\n        // Update table rows\n        d3.selectAll('#stock-table tbody tr').classed('selected', d => d.ticker === state.selectedTicker);\n\n        // Scroll table row into view if selected\n        if (state.selectedTicker) {\n            const selectedRow = document.querySelector(`tr[data-ticker=\"${state.selectedTicker}\"]`);\n            if (selectedRow) {\n '#999';\n    }\n\n    // Format market cap as human-readable\n    function formatMarketCap(value) {\n        if (!value || value === null || value === undefined) return 'N/A';\n\n        const absValue = Math.abs(value);\n\n        if (absValue >= 1e12) {\n            return (value / 1e12).toFixed(2) + 'T';\n        } else if (absValue >= 1e9) {\n            return (value / 1e9).toFixed(2) }\n\n        return value.toFixed(2);\n    }\n\n    // Calculate bubble radius based on market cap\n    function calculateRadius(marketCap, allCaps) {\n        if (!marketCap || marketCap === null) {\n            return 25; // Fixed size for ETFs\n        }\n\n        const maxCap = Math.max(...allCaps.filter(m => m && m > 0));\n        const minCap = Math.min(...allCaps.filter(m => m && m > 0));\n        const range = maxCap - minCap;\n\n        const normalized = (marketCap - minCap) / range;\n        const radius = 20 + normalized * 60; // Between 20 and 80 pixels\n        return radius;\n    }\n\n    // Load and process data\n    async function loadData() {\n        try {\n            const stocks = await d3.csv('data/stock-descriptions.csv');\n\n            // Process stock .attr('transform', `translate(${config.margin.left},${config.margin.top})`);\n\n        // Get unique sectors\n        const sectors = [...new Set(stocks.map(d => d.sector))];\n\n        // Position X centers for each sector (spread across width)\n        const sectorCenters = {};\n        sectors.forEach((sector, i) => {\n            sectorCenters[sector] = (width / (sectors.length + 1)) * (i + .enter()\n            .append('text')\n            .attr('text-anchor', 'middle')\n            .attr('dy', '0.35em')\n            .attr('x', d => d.x || width / 2)\n            .attr('y', d => d.y || }\n\n        function hideTooltip() {\n            tooltip.transition().duration(500).style('opacity', 0);\n        }\n\n        // Update positions on simulation tick\n        simulation.on('tick', d.y)));\n\n            labels\n                .attr('x', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(width - d.radius, d.x)))\n                .attr('y', d => Math.max(d.radius, Math.min(height - d.radius, d.y)));\n        });\n\n        // Return elements for later reference\n        return { svg, g, circles, labels, tooltip, width, height };\n    }\n\n    // Create data table\n    function .append('tr')\n            .attr('data-ticker', d => d.ticker)\n            .on('click', function(event, d) {\n                selectStock(d.ticker);\n            });\n\n        rows.append('td')\n Larger bubbles represent companies with larger market caps\n  - ETFs are displayed with uniform size (no market cap data available)\n- **Bubble Color**: Determined by sector\n  - Each sector gets a distinct color\n  - Legend provided below the chart\n- **Bubble Position**: Organized by force simulation\n  - Bubbles cluster by sector using D3.js force simulation\n  - `forceX/forceY` attracts bubbles toward sector-specific positions\n  - `forceCollide` prevents overlapping bubbles\n  - `forceManyBody` provides repulsive force for better spacing\n- **Bubble Label**: Ticker symbol displayed inside each bubble\n- **Hover Tooltip**: Shows ticker, company name, and sector (not for ETFs)\n  - Appears on mouse hover\n  - Positioned near cursor\n\n### Data Table (Right Side)\n- **Columns**: Ticker, Company Name, Sector, Market Cap\n- **Market Cap Formatting**: Human-readable format (T for trillion, B for billion, M for million)\n- **Row Highlighting**:\n  - Hover rows for visual feedback\n  - Selected row highlighted in blue\n- **Sticky Header**: Table header remains visible while scrolling\n\n### Interactivity\n\n#### Click to Select\n- Click any bubble to highlight it in both chart and table\n- Click a table row to highlight the corresponding bubble\n- Click again to deselect (toggle behavior)\n- Click background/outside to deselect all\n\n#### Visual Feedback\n- Selected items highlighted with blue background\n- Hovered items have slight background color change\n- Smooth transitions on all interactions\n\n### Responsive Design\n- Side-by-side layout on desktop (1200px+)\n- Stacked layout on tablets (768px-1200px)\n- Single column on mobile (<768px)\n- All elements scale appropriately\n\n## How to Use\n\n1. **Open in Browser**\n   ```bash\n   # Navigate to the output directory and open in a web server\n   cd /root/output\n   python3 -m http.server 8000\n   # Then open http://localhost:8000 in your browser\n   ```\n\n2. **Explore Stocks**\n   - Hover over bubbles to see company details\n   - Bubbles of the same color belong to the same sector\n   - Larger bubbles represent more valuable companies\n\n3. **Find Specific Stock**\n   - Scroll through the data table on the right\n   - Click a row to find its bubble on the chart\n   - Use your browser's find function (Ctrl+F or Cmd+F) in the table\n\n4. **Compare Markets**\n   - Notice how sectors cluster together on the chart\n   - Table can be sorted by column (click header) if needed\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\n/root/output/\n├── index.html                 # Main HTML file\n├── css/\n│   └── style.css             # Styling and responsive layout\n├── js/\n│   ├── d3.v6.min.js          # D3.js v6 library (265KB)\n│   └── visualization.js      # Main visualization code\n├── data/\n│   ├── stock-descriptions.csv # Stock metadata\n│   └── indiv-stock/          # Individual stock price histories\n│       ├── AAPL.csv\n│       ├── MSFT.csv\n│       └── ...  /* Change to desired height */\n}\n```\n\n## Browser Compatibility\n- Chrome/Chromium: ✓ Full support\n- Firefox: ✓ Full support\n- Safari: ✓ Full support\n- Edge: ✓ Full support\n- IE11: ✗ Not supported (uses ES6 features)\n\n## Performance Notes\n- Loads 50 stocks + 50 stock history files (~2-5 MB total)\n- Force simulation runs smoothly on modern computers\n- SVG rendering used (scales well, no pixelation)\n- Smooth scrolling for table with sticky header\n\n## Data Source\nStock data includes:\n- Ticker symbols\n- Company names\n- Sector classification\n- Market capitalization\n- Employment figures\n- Financial metrics (ROE, revenue per share, book value, yield, beta)\n- Business summaries\n- Country and website information\n\n## Notes\n- ETF entries have no market cap data, so they're sized uniformly\n- Tooltips only show for stocks with market cap data (not ETFs)\n- Table is sortable by default in some browsers\n- Responsive design works on all modern devices\n\n## License\nThis visualization uses D3.js v6 (BSD 3-Clause License)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":197,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":44900,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"72c189fa-d551-409c-b661-6278d6e9803c","uuid":"232f441a-5d14-430f-b548-72d64f488857"}
